	<script>
	(function() {
		function getCookie(name) {
			var match = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(?:^|; )' + name.replace(/([.$?*|{}()\[\]\\\/+^])/g, '\\$1') + '=([^;]*)'));
			return match ? decodeURIComponent(match[1]) : '';
		}
		function setCookie(name, value, days) {
			if (!value) return;
			var expires = "";
			if (days) {
				var d = new Date();
				d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days*24*60*60*1000));
				expires = "; expires=" + d.toUTCString();
			}
			var cookie = name + "=" + encodeURIComponent(value) + expires + "; path=/; SameSite=Lax";
			if (location.protocol === 'https:') cookie += "; Secure";
			document.cookie = cookie;
		}

		function extractGclidFromGl(glVal) {
			if (!glVal) return "";
			try {
				var dec = decodeURIComponent(glVal);
				var parts = dec.split('*');
				for (var i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) {
					// Priority: gclid, then gbraid, then wbraid (if you ever want them later)
					if (parts[i] === 'gclid' || parts[i] === 'gbraid' || parts[i] === 'wbraid') {
						return parts[i+1] || "";
					}
				}
			} catch (e) {
				// ignore decode failures
			}
			return "";
		}

		var params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);

		// 1) First-visit URL: only set if not already present
		if (!getCookie('leansai_first_url') && !window.location.pathname.startsWith('/wp-admin')) {
			var url = window.location.href;
			if (url.length > 2000) url = url.substring(0, 2000);
			setCookie('leansai_first_url', url, 365);
		}

		// 2) GCLID chain: force -> gclid param -> _gl fallback
		var newGclid = "";
		var force = params.get('leansai_force_gclid');
		if (force) {
			newGclid = force;
		} else {
			var gclidParam = params.get('gclid');
			if (gclidParam) {
				newGclid = gclidParam;
			} else {
				var glParam = params.get('_gl');
				var fromGl = extractGclidFromGl(glParam);
				if (fromGl) {
					newGclid = fromGl;
				}
			}
		}

		if (newGclid) {
			setCookie('leansai_gclid', newGclid, 365);
		}
	})();
	</script>

/* =========================================================
   LEANS.AI PRIMARY CTA BUTTON

   CANONICAL CLASS: .leans-button-primary
   --------------------------------------
   Edit THIS block whenever the primary CTA design changes.

   ELEMENTOR:
   Add "leans-button-primary" under Advanced > CSS Classes on
   an Elementor Button widget. Elementor applies that class to
   the widget wrapper, so the selectors below intentionally target
   the nested .elementor-button element.

   DIRECT HTML:
   The canonical class also works when placed directly on an
   <a> or <button> element.

   LEGACY ALIASES:
   .button_remi_cta
   .button_remi_cta_mobile

   These legacy classes remain here so older shortcodes, templates,
   and page content automatically receive the current design.
   Do not create separate styling blocks for the legacy classes.
   ========================================================= */

    <script>
    window.addEventListener('load', function () {

      try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('wdEventStarted')); } catch(e) {}

      var startedAt = Date.now();
      var maxMs = 3000;         // hard stop after 3 seconds
      var tickMs = 150;         // check a bit faster but for less time
      var didFire = false;

      var interval = setInterval(function () {
        // hard timeout
        if (Date.now() - startedAt > maxMs) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          return;
        }

        // wait for jQuery + header ready
        if (window.jQuery && jQuery('header.whb-header').hasClass('whb-sticky-prepared')) {
          if (!didFire) {
            didFire = true;
            jQuery(window).trigger('scroll');
          }
          clearInterval(interval);
        }
      }, tickMs);

    });
    </script>

<script>
  // Show "… See more" only when clamped; toggle expand/collapse
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.review-card').forEach(function(card){
      const text = card.querySelector('.review-text');
      const btn  = card.querySelector('.review-toggle');
      if (!text || !btn) return;

      // Wait a tick for layout, then check overflow
      requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        const needsToggle = text.scrollHeight > text.clientHeight + 1;
        if (needsToggle) btn.hidden = false;
      });

      btn.addEventListener('click', function(){
        const expanded = card.classList.toggle('is-expanded');
        btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', expanded ? 'true' : 'false');
        btn.textContent = expanded ? 'See less' : '… See more';
      });
    });
  });
</script>

        <script id="leans-scorecard-js">(function () {
    'use strict';

    function initScorecard(scorecard) {
        if (!scorecard || scorecard.dataset.scorecardInitialized === '1') {
            return;
        }

        scorecard.dataset.scorecardInitialized = '1';

        const tabs = Array.from(
            scorecard.querySelectorAll('[data-scorecard-tab]')
        );

        const panels = Array.from(
            scorecard.querySelectorAll('[data-scorecard-panel]')
        );

        if (!tabs.length || !panels.length) {
            return;
        }

        function setMode(mode, focusTab) {
            tabs.forEach(function (tab) {
                const active = tab.dataset.scorecardTab === mode;

                tab.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
                tab.setAttribute('aria-selected', active ? 'true' : 'false');
                tab.setAttribute('tabindex', active ? '0' : '-1');

                if (active && focusTab) {
                    tab.focus();
                }
            });

            panels.forEach(function (panel) {
                const active = panel.dataset.scorecardPanel === mode;

                panel.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
                panel.hidden = !active;
            });
        }

        tabs.forEach(function (tab, index) {
            tab.addEventListener('click', function () {
                setMode(this.dataset.scorecardTab, false);
            });

            tab.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
                if (
                    event.key !== 'ArrowLeft' &&
                    event.key !== 'ArrowRight'
                ) {
                    return;
                }

                event.preventDefault();

                const delta = event.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1;
                const nextIndex = (index + delta + tabs.length) % tabs.length;
                const nextTab = tabs[nextIndex];

                setMode(nextTab.dataset.scorecardTab, true);
            });
        });

        const initial = scorecard.dataset.scorecardDefault === 'exec'
            ? 'exec'
            : 'vault';

        setMode(initial, false);
    }

    function initAllScorecards(root) {
        (root || document)
            .querySelectorAll('.leans-scorecard')
            .forEach(initScorecard);
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            initAllScorecards(document);
        });
    } else {
        initAllScorecards(document);
    }

    window.addEventListener('elementor/frontend/init', function () {
        initAllScorecards(document);
    });

    if (typeof MutationObserver !== 'undefined') {
        const observer = new MutationObserver(function (mutations) {
            const hasAddedNodes = mutations.some(function (mutation) {
                return mutation.addedNodes && mutation.addedNodes.length;
            });

            if (hasAddedNodes) {
                initAllScorecards(document);
            }
        });

        observer.observe(document.documentElement, {
            childList: true,
            subtree: true
        });
    }
})();
</script></div>

<section

        <!-- =================================================

        <header class="leans-faq__header">

            <div class="leans-faq__eyebrow">
                Leans.AI FAQ
            </div>

            <h2
                id="leans-faq-title"
                class="leans-faq__title"
            >
                Common Questions
            </h2>

            <div class="leans-faq__rule"></div>

        <!-- =================================================
             FAQ COLUMNS
        ================================================== -->

        <div class="leans-faq__columns">


            <!-- =================================================
                 LEFT COLUMN
            ================================================== -->

            <div class="leans-faq__column">


                <!-- FAQ 1 -->

                <details class="leans-faq__item">

                    <summary class="leans-faq__question">
                        If REMI's™ Picks Are So Good - Why Do You Sell Them?

                        <span
                            class="leans-faq__toggle"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>
                    </summary>

                    <div class="leans-faq__answer">
                        <p>
                            On multiple occasions members of our team have been blacklisted or cut-off by sportsbooks from making too much money too quickly. Although this is rare, the first time it happened it forced us to think bigger than just using Remi for our own strategies, so we started selling Remi’s data.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                </details>


                <!-- FAQ 2 -->

                <details class="leans-faq__item">

                    <summary class="leans-faq__question">
                        Does REMI™ Offer New AI Sports Betting Picks Every Day?

                        <span
                            class="leans-faq__toggle"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>
                    </summary>

                    <div class="leans-faq__answer">
                        <p>
                            Yes. Remi generates fresh AI sports picks daily across NFL, CFB, NBA, CBB, NHL, and MLB. You can expect 2–6 picks on a typical day, and up to 10 selections on busy days. Each pick includes win probability, confidence units, and odds. Free and premium picks are always posted on our Picks page.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                </details>


                <!-- FAQ 3 -->

                <details class="leans-faq__item">

                    <summary class="leans-faq__question">
                        How Many AI Sports Picks Does REMI™ Provide Each Day?

                        <span
                            class="leans-faq__toggle"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>
                    </summary>

                    <div class="leans-faq__answer">
                        <p>
                            On average, Remi releases 2–6 daily picks. During peak sports seasons, the model may provide more selections, sometimes reaching 10 in a day. Every pick comes with probability, units, and odds so you can evaluate risk and value. Visit our AI Picks hub to see today’s plays.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                </details>


                <!-- FAQ 4 -->

                <details class="leans-faq__item">

                    <summary class="leans-faq__question">
                        What Sports Does REMI™ Analyze?

                        <span
                            class="leans-faq__toggle"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>
                    </summary>

                    <div class="leans-faq__answer">
                        <p>
                            Remi covers the six biggest sports: NFL, College Football, NBA, College Basketball, NHL, and MLB. Each sport has its own AI-powered prediction model and dedicated page for odds comparisons and picks. Remi updates 24/7 so you always see the latest recommendations.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                </details>


                <!-- FAQ 5 -->

                <details class="leans-faq__item">

                    <summary class="leans-faq__question">
                        How Accurate Is REMI™ Compared to Human Handicappers?

                        <span
                            class="leans-faq__toggle"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>
                    </summary>

                    <div class="leans-faq__answer">
                        <p>
                            Most handicappers struggle to maintain long-term success. Remi’s 53–58% historical win rate across major sports consistently outperforms typical human handicappers, who usually hover closer to 50%. While no model wins every bet, Remi’s advanced AI algorithms provide a reliable edge over conventional methods.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                </details>


                <!-- FAQ 6 -->

                <details class="leans-faq__item">

                    <summary class="leans-faq__question">
                        Are All of REMI’s Picks Free?

                        <span
                            class="leans-faq__toggle"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>
                    </summary>

                    <div class="leans-faq__answer">
                        <p>
                            Leans.AI provides a daily free pick, but most AI picks are available through premium subscription plans. Free users can sample the model’s accuracy, while subscribers gain access to the full slate of daily AI predictions, unit confidence ratings, and parlay/prop insights.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                </details>


                <!-- FAQ 7 -->

                <details class="leans-faq__item">

                    <summary class="leans-faq__question">
                        How Do I Get Started With Leans.AI?

                        <span
                            class="leans-faq__toggle"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>
                    </summary>

                    <div class="leans-faq__answer">
                        <p>
                            Simply click ‘Get Free Picks Now’ to create an account and view today’s free AI pick. For full access, choose a subscription plan that fits your needs. Setup takes less than two minutes, and once subscribed, you’ll receive daily AI picks for NFL, NBA, MLB, NHL, and college sports, directly through the site and email.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                </details>


                <!-- FAQ 8 -->

                <details class="leans-faq__item">

                    <summary class="leans-faq__question">
                        What Are The Benefits Of Using AI Sports Picks?

                        <span
                            class="leans-faq__toggle"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>
                    </summary>

                    <div class="leans-faq__answer">
                        <p>
                            AI is free of human emotion, and owing to its self-learning algorithm, its winning percentage will often increase over time.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                </details>

Pricing is competitive with traditional handicappers, and subscriptions can be managed directly on the site.
                        </p>

                </details>

                        </p>
                    </div>

                <!-- FAQ 16 -->

                <details class="leans-faq__item">

                    <summary class="leans-faq__question">
                        What Is A Unit?

                        <span
                            class="leans-faq__toggle"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>
                    </summary>

                    <div class="leans-faq__answer">
                        <p>
                            A Unit is calculated on a mathematical formula (we use a modified Kelly Criterion approach) correlating expected win probability and payout odds. This is also referred to ‘EV’, but for most purpose we’ll use a ‘Unit’.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                </details>


                <!-- FAQ 17 -->

                <details class="leans-faq__item">

                        ></span>

                        </p>

<script>
(function () {

    var faq =
        document.querySelector('.leans-faq');

    if (!faq) {
        return;
    }

    var items =
        faq.querySelectorAll(
            '.leans-faq__item'
        );

    items.forEach(function (item) {

        item.addEventListener(
            'toggle',
            function () {

                if (!item.open) {
                    return;
                }

                items.forEach(
                    function (other) {

                        if (
                            other !== item &&
                            other.open
                        ) {
                            other.open = false;
                        }

                    }
                );

            }
        );

    });

})();
</script>				</div>

<script>
(function () {

    function updateFinalCTA() {

        var button =
            document.getElementById(
                'leans-final-cta-button'
            );

        if (!button) {
            return;
        }

        if (
            document.body &&
            document.body.classList.contains(
                'logged-in'
            )
        ) {

            button.href =
                'https://leans.ai/upgrade/';

            button.textContent =
                'EXPLORE PREMIUM ACCESS';

            button.classList.add(
                'is-premium'
            );

        }

    }

    if (
        document.readyState === 'loading'
    ) {

        document.addEventListener(
            'DOMContentLoaded',
            updateFinalCTA
        );

    } else {

        updateFinalCTA();

    }

})();
</script>				</div>

</div><script>
jQuery(function($){
  // fire when Woo AJAX add-to-cart finishes
  $(document.body).on('added_to_cart', function(e, fragments, cart_hash, $button){
    if(!$button || !$button.length) return;

    var pid   = String($button.data('product_id') || '');
    var sku   = $button.data('product_sku') || pid;
    var name  = $button.data('product_name') || '';
    var qty   = parseInt($button.data('quantity') || 1, 10);
    var price = parseFloat($button.data('price') || 0);
    var currency = "USD";

    // de-dupe per click
    var flag = 'ga4_atc_' + pid + '_' + qty + '_' + cart_hash;
    if (sessionStorage.getItem(flag)) return;

    window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
    dataLayer.push({
      event: "add_to_cart",
      ecommerce: {
        currency: currency,
        value: price ? +(price * qty).toFixed(2) : undefined,
        items: [{
          item_id: sku,
          item_name: name,
          price: price || undefined,
          quantity: qty
        }]
      }
    });
    sessionStorage.setItem(flag, "1");

    // redirect AFTER we push to dataLayer (ensures GTM can fire)
    if ($button.hasClass('redirect_after_atc')) {
      setTimeout(function(){ window.location.href = "/checkout-8/"; }, 250);
    }
  });
});
</script>
			<script>
				( () => {
					const lazyloadRunObserver = () => {
						const lazyloadBackgrounds = document.querySelectorAll( `.e-con.e-parent:not(.e-lazyloaded)` );
						const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
							entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
								if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
									let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
									if( lazyloadBackground ) {
										lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
									}
									lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
								}
							});
						}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
						lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
							lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
						} );
					};
					const events = [
						'DOMContentLoaded',
						'elementor/lazyload/observe',
					];
					events.forEach( ( event ) => {
						document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );
					} );
				} )();
			</script>

<script id="cmplz-cookiebanner-js-after">
		if ('undefined' != typeof window.jQuery) {
			jQuery(document).ready(function ($) {
				$(document).on('elementor/popup/show', () => {
					let rev_cats = cmplz_categories.reverse();
					for (let key in rev_cats) {
						if (rev_cats.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
							let category = cmplz_categories[key];
							if (cmplz_has_consent(category)) {
								document.querySelectorAll('[data-category="' + category + '"]').forEach(obj => {
									cmplz_remove_placeholder(obj);
								});
							}
						}
					}

					let services = cmplz_get_services_on_page();
					for (let key in services) {
						if (services.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
							let service = services[key].service;
							let category = services[key].category;
							if (cmplz_has_service_consent(service, category)) {
								document.querySelectorAll('[data-service="' + service + '"]').forEach(obj => {
									cmplz_remove_placeholder(obj);
								});
							}
						}
					}
				});
			});
		}
    
    
		
			document.addEventListener("cmplz_enable_category", function(consentData) {
				var category = consentData.detail.category;
				var services = consentData.detail.services;
				var blockedContentContainers = [];
				let selectorVideo = '.cmplz-elementor-widget-video-playlist[data-category="'+category+'"],.elementor-widget-video[data-category="'+category+'"]';
				let selectorGeneric = '[data-cmplz-elementor-href][data-category="'+category+'"]';
				for (var skey in services) {
					if (services.hasOwnProperty(skey)) {
						let service = skey;
						selectorVideo +=',.cmplz-elementor-widget-video-playlist[data-service="'+service+'"],.elementor-widget-video[data-service="'+service+'"]';
						selectorGeneric +=',[data-cmplz-elementor-href][data-service="'+service+'"]';
					}
				}
				document.querySelectorAll(selectorVideo).forEach(obj => {
					let elementService = obj.getAttribute('data-service');
					if ( cmplz_is_service_denied(elementService) ) {
						return;
					}
					if (obj.classList.contains('cmplz-elementor-activated')) return;
					obj.classList.add('cmplz-elementor-activated');

					if ( obj.hasAttribute('data-cmplz_elementor_widget_type') ){
						let attr = obj.getAttribute('data-cmplz_elementor_widget_type');
						obj.classList.removeAttribute('data-cmplz_elementor_widget_type');
						obj.classList.setAttribute('data-widget_type', attr);
					}
					if (obj.classList.contains('cmplz-elementor-widget-video-playlist')) {
						obj.classList.remove('cmplz-elementor-widget-video-playlist');
						obj.classList.add('elementor-widget-video-playlist');
					}
					obj.setAttribute('data-settings', obj.getAttribute('data-cmplz-elementor-settings'));
					blockedContentContainers.push(obj);
				});

				document.querySelectorAll(selectorGeneric).forEach(obj => {
					let elementService = obj.getAttribute('data-service');
					if ( cmplz_is_service_denied(elementService) ) {
						return;
					}
					if (obj.classList.contains('cmplz-elementor-activated')) return;

					if (obj.classList.contains('cmplz-fb-video')) {
						obj.classList.remove('cmplz-fb-video');
						obj.classList.add('fb-video');
					}

					obj.classList.add('cmplz-elementor-activated');
					obj.setAttribute('data-href', obj.getAttribute('data-cmplz-elementor-href'));
					blockedContentContainers.push(obj.closest('.elementor-widget'));
				});

				/**
				 * Trigger the widgets in Elementor
				 */
				for (var key in blockedContentContainers) {
					if (blockedContentContainers.hasOwnProperty(key) && blockedContentContainers[key] !== undefined) {
						let blockedContentContainer = blockedContentContainers[key];
						if (elementorFrontend.elementsHandler) {
							elementorFrontend.elementsHandler.runReadyTrigger(blockedContentContainer)
						}
						var cssIndex = blockedContentContainer.getAttribute('data-placeholder_class_index');
						blockedContentContainer.classList.remove('cmplz-blocked-content-container');
						blockedContentContainer.classList.remove('cmplz-placeholder-' + cssIndex);
					}
				}

			});

const revokeListeners = [];
window.addRevokeListener = (callback) => {
	revokeListeners.push(callback);
};
document.addEventListener("cmplz_revoke", function (e) {
	cmplz_set_cookie('cmplz_consent_mode', 'revoked', false );
	revokeListeners.forEach((callback) => {
		callback();
	});
});

const consentListeners = [];
/**
 * Called from GTM template to set callback to be executed when user consent is provided.
 * @param callback
 */
window.addConsentUpdateListener = (callback) => {
	consentListeners.push(callback);
};
document.addEventListener("cmplz_fire_categories", function (e) {
	var consentedCategories = e.detail.categories;
	const consent = {
		'security_storage': "granted",
		'functionality_storage': "granted",
		'personalization_storage':  cmplz_in_array( 'preferences', consentedCategories ) ? 'granted' : 'denied',
		'analytics_storage':  cmplz_in_array( 'statistics', consentedCategories ) ? 'granted' : 'denied',
		'ad_storage': cmplz_in_array( 'marketing', consentedCategories ) ? 'granted' : 'denied',
		'ad_user_data': cmplz_in_array( 'marketing', consentedCategories ) ? 'granted' : 'denied',
		'ad_personalization': cmplz_in_array( 'marketing', consentedCategories ) ? 'granted' : 'denied',
	};

	//don't use automatic prefixing, as the TM template needs to be sure it's cmplz_.
	let consented = [];
	for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(consent)) {
		if (value === 'granted') {
			consented.push(key);
		}
	}
	cmplz_set_cookie('cmplz_consent_mode', consented.join(','), false );
	consentListeners.forEach((callback) => {
		callback(consent);
	});
});
